{

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_011qDnRmfTfZg18F2dzaZE4h`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

Now I have the full picture for the 3B aligned eval matrix:

**3B s123 aligned eval**:
- d000: ✅ (exp_001_eval_3B_d000_s123_aligned, mmlu=0.6332, gsm8k=0.1812, arc=0.8328)
- d005: 🏃 RUNNING (exp_001_eval_3B_d005_s123_aligned, cuda:2)
- d025: 📊 ANALYZING (exp_001_eval_3B_d025_s123_aligned, just completed, cuda:3 now free)


- d100: ✅ (exp_001_eval_3B_d100_v2_s123_aligned, mmlu=0.6319, gsm8k=0.7324, arc=0.8311)

The s42 aligned eval is also progressing with d000 and d005 both completed, showing similar performance patterns across the benchmark metrics.

Once the remaining s123 evals finish, that evaluation matrix will be complete. For s42, the evaluations are done but I need to rerun the SFTs with a higher alpha value of 32, and then generate new evaluations against those updated checkpoints. I'm currently running those SFT reruns on the available GPU.

Now cuda:3 is free and I need to decide what to prioritize next—either an evaluation that's ready to run or an SFT that's needed downstream.

Looking at the current status, the 3B s123 evals are mostly done or already running, and the D055 SFTs are in progress. But exp_008_instruct_rl_proxy is sitting in PREPARING state—it's marked as P0 in the experiment plan and only requires inference on Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct, so it could be a good fit for cuda:3 right now. Though I should verify what this experiment actually entails since the registry shows it separately from the signal_survival_analysis task listed in the plan.

The experiment is about using the SFT+RL post-trained Instruct model as a data point in a 3-regime comparison (base pretrained → d000 SFT → Instruct SFT+RL), computing all 4 signals on the Instruct model to observe how signal values change across training regimes. No additional training is needed, just signal computation. But this depends on the exp-002 signal pipeline being ready, and the 3B signal computation hasn't started yet, so this might be blocked.

I need to figure out the best use of cuda:3 right now—either run one of the remaining 0.5B evals still in PREPARING, wait for the current SFTs to finish and then queue up a new eval, or just report the GPU as free for now.

Looking at the PREPARING list, there are two 0.5B evals that are small and could finish quickly, but they've had many failed predecessors. I think the right move is to report the eval completion to the Director, flag cuda:3 as available, and let them decide what to run next—though I should probably suggest something based on the roadmap priorities rather than leaving it completely open. The coverage collapse investigation is the highest priority, followed by completing seed=123 for N≥16 pooled experiments.

The 3B s123 evals are nearly done, and once d005 finishes, I'll have all the aligned evals for that seed. After that, the next high-impact work would be signal computation for the 3B models. For cuda:3 right now, I could either run one of the pending 0.5B or 1.5B experiments, or wait for the D055 SFTs to finish and immediately start their evals. I'll report to the Director with the eval results, GPU status, and a concrete suggestion for what cuda:3 should tackle next, but first let me check on the worker status since it's been a while.

  "content": "# 项目关键发现与决策记录\n\n## ⚡ exp-004 核心结论：Coverage collapse = Model misspecification（2026-05-15）\n\nSimulation sweep（66/80 seeds）证明：DGP=模型时 CI95 coverage 90-100%（即使 γ=4.0）。真实数据 coverage 仅 20-33%。\n→ **coverage collapse 不是 γ 值大导致，而是 additive 4PL+γ 模型与真实 contamination 机制的 misspecification。**\n这支持 \"benchmark-dependent effects\" framing，coverage collapse 可作为 model misspecification 的诊断证据。\n\n## Benchmark Contamination 敏感性分层（2026-05-15）\n\n3B s123 aligned eval 数据（d000→d025→d100）揭示明确分层：\n- **高敏感**: GSM8K（d000=0.18→d025=0.53→d100=0.73，+34pp@25%剂量）、HumanEval（d000=0.09→d025=0.16→d100=0.18）\n- **不敏感**: MMLU（<0.5pp 变化）、ARC（<0.3pp 变化）\n\n**为什么重要**: MMLU/ARC 是天然对照组，contamination 对它们几乎无影响。这支持 per-benchmark γ_k 参数化——γ_GSM8K 会远大于 γ_MMLU。也意味着论文应强调 benchmark-specific effects 而非 global contamination factor。\n\n## 1.5B 跨种子一致性（2026-05-15）\n\nd025 cross-seed (s42 vs s123, aligned): MMLU Δ=0.21pp, Self-Critique entropy collapse\n\n0.5B 和 1.5B 的 signals 已完成。3B 未开始（之前 v2 因 GPU 资源冲突被 kill，非代码问题）。\n这是 Claim 2 的关键瓶颈（coverage 0%）。\n\n## 步骤\n\n1. SSH (read_only) 到 cqa1-21977，检查 signal computation 代码：\n```bash\nls /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/\nfind /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/ -name \"*.py\" | head -20\n```\n\n2. 检查 0.5B/1.5B 的 signal 输出格式和目录结构，作为 3B 的参考：\n```bash\nls /root/autodl-tmp/exp002/ 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_DIR\"\nfind /root/autodl-tmp/ -path \"*/signals*\" -o -path \"*/exp002*\" 2>/dev/null | head -20\n```\n\n3. 验证 3B s123 SFT checkpoints 全部就绪（4 dosages × s123）：\n```bash\nfor d in d000 d005 d025 d100; do\n  ls /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Qwen2.5-3B_${d}_s123/lora_adapter/ 2>/dev/null && echo \"${d}_s123: OK\" || echo \"${d}_s123: MISSING\"\ndone\n```\n\n4. 检查 signal 脚本的 help 信息或 main entry point：\n```bash\ncat /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/*.py | head -100\n# 或者\npython /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/compute_signals.py --help 2>/dev/null\n```\n\n5. 检查之前 0.5B/1.5B signals 的成功运行命令（从 registry 中查找）\n\n6. 在最终输出中报告：\n   - 代码是否就绪\n   - 所有 prerequisites 是否满足\n   - 建议的提交命令（适配 3B + cuda:3）\n   - 预估运行时间\n   - 任何潜在问题或依赖\n\n## 重要\n- 只用 ssh_read_only 读信息，不要修改任何东西\n- 不要实际提交实验\n- 详细记录发现，方便后续直接提交\n",

[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
&lt;/handoff>

[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
